# Range-break + 1-minute displacement: every search table (owner, 2026-10-21)

yrs+ = full years 2022-25 with a positive net. Strategies: draft_nq_disp_range_lab / _fade / _ovn, draft_es_disp_range_lab, draft_gc_disp_range_lab.

# Frequency round (owner, 2026-10-21): longer windows, session blocks, afternoon, overnight

All IN-SAMPLE (the 3-month pick windows of the 3:1 block walk-forward, 883 NQ session days, 2021-10 .. 2026-04). Test months stored, not shown.
Rule: 1-minute candle closes beyond the range AND beyond the previous 1-minute candle, body >= k x ATR(14) (optional volume >= 3x the 20-candle mean),
stop at the candle's far end, target 1:2, one position at a time. Edge = win% minus break-even of the realized RR; the bar is +3. Per-run grids hold every box (top 8 with >= 80 trades shown).
